# Rosetta Code tasks

Every task and draft task on [Rosetta Code](https://rosettacode.org), listed
from its MediaWiki API on 2026-10-15. Each name links to the task page. The Ada
and SPARK columns link to the solution in that language when the page has one.
The Local column links to the downloaded copy in [rosetta/](rosetta/README.md): Ada
where the page has it, otherwise FreeBASIC or Phix.

The Silver column gives each task's verdict for Silver as the [Oracle](oracle.md)
envisions it and links to its assessment. A candidate shows its position in the
[progression](rosetta/progression.md), the order in which Silver could grow to
support it, and whether its proofs would likely or plausibly discharge
automatically. Other tasks are unlikely to prove automatically or are excluded,
with the reasons. Design-pending tasks have no stage until a faithful program
is established. The assessments and the inclusion policy are in
[rosetta/coverage.json](rosetta/coverage.json).

| Status | Tasks | With an Ada solution | With a SPARK solution | Likely | Plausible | Unlikely | Excluded | Design pending |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Task | 1,362 | 971 | 8 | 552 | 645 | 1 | 164 | 0 |
| Draft | 420 | 123 | 0 | 194 | 185 | 1 | 39 | 1 |
| Total | 1,782 | 1,094 | 8 | 746 | 830 | 2 | 203 | 1 |

Rosetta Code marks 13 tasks as omitted from Ada. The Ada column says
"omitted" for 12 of them. The other,
[Extend your language](https://rosettacode.org/wiki/Extend_your_language),
has an Ada solution anyway.

The task lists come from the categories
[Programming Tasks](https://rosettacode.org/wiki/Category:Programming_Tasks) and
[Draft Programming Tasks](https://rosettacode.org/wiki/Category:Draft_Programming_Tasks).
The solution columns come from each page's language headings at the revision
recorded in [rosetta/manifest.json](rosetta/manifest.json). To refresh this list
and the local copy, run `python3 rosetta/refresh.py`.

Rosetta Code content, including solution code, is available under the GNU Free
Documentation License 1.3.
